L1645: **Reviewer's findings:** Based on the criteria of adjustment for multiple testing discussed in the mouse data analysis section, we make the following conclusions:
L1646: - For male mice, the pairwise comparisons between the vehicle control and the positive control showed statistically significant increases in incidence of Lymphoma in Hemolympho-reticu (P-value&lt;0.001), Squamous cell Papilloma in Stomach, nonglandu (P-value&lt;0.001), Squamous cell Carcinoma in Stomach, nonglandu (P-value=0.0071), Combined tumors of Squamous cell Papilloma+Carcinoma in Skin/Subcutis (P-value=0.0423).
- For male mice, the pairwise comparisons between the water control and the positive control showed statistically significant increases in incidence of Lymphoma in Hemolympho-reticu (P-value&lt;0.001), Squamous cell Papilloma in Stomach, nonglandu (P-value&lt;0.001), Squamous cell Carcinoma in Stomach, nonglandu (P-value=0.0071), Combined tumors of Squamous cell Papilloma+Carcinoma in Skin/Subcutis (P-value=0.0423).
- For female mice, the pairwise comparisons between the vehicle control and the positive control showed statistically significant increases in incidence of Lymphoma in Hemolympho-reticu (P-value&lt;0.001), Squamous cell Papilloma in Stomach, nonglandu (P-value&lt;0.001), Squamous cell Papilloma in Skin/Subcutis (P-value=0.0345), Combined tumors of Squamous cell Papilloma+Carcinoma in Skin/Subcutis (P-value=0.0345).
- For female mice, the pairwise comparisons between the water control and the positive control showed statistically significant increases in incidence of Lymphoma in Hemolympho-reticu (P-value&lt;0.001), Squamous cell Papilloma in Stomach, nonglandu (P-value&lt;0.001), Squamous cell Papilloma in Skin/Subcutis (P-value=0.0369), Combined tumors of Squamous cell Papilloma+Carcinoma in Skin/Subcutis (P-value=0.0369).
L1647: ## 4. Conclusion
L1648: In this submission the sponsor included the report of one animal carcinogenicity study 001178-T (hemizygous) RasH2 mice. This study was intended to assess the carcinogenic potential of GBT440 when administered orally by gavage at appropriate drug levels for 26 weeks in mice.
L1649: **Mouse Study:** One hundred and twenty five RasH2 mice of each sex were randomly assigned to the treated, vehicle and water control groups in equal size of 25 mice per group. There are 10 mice of each sex in the positive control group. The dose levels for treated the GBT44 groups were 50, 150, and 500 mg/kg/day. The mice in the vehicle control group received the vehicle (0.5% (w/w) Methocel\pm E50 in reverse osmosis water containing 0.01% (w/w) polysorbate 80 and 10 mM phosphate buffer (pH 7 \pm 0.2)). The mice in the water control group received the reverse osmosis water. The mice in the positive control group received acidified physiological saline (150 mM sodium chloride and 15 mM sodium citrate in reverse osmosis water, adjusted
L1650: to pH 4.5 \pm 0.1 with 1 N hydrochloric acid) injection once on Day 1 at the dose of 75 mg/kg. The study was designed to continue for up to 26 weeks for both sexes, however in accordance with study termination criteria, all surviving mice were sacrificed during Week 27.
L1651: Survival analysis showed that no GBT440-related effects on survival or mortality occurred. The pairwise comparisons showed statistically significant increases in mortality in the positive control group when compared to the vehicle control and to the water control for the male and female mice. For male mice, the p-values for Likelihood Ratio test are 0.0016 and 0.0002 and the p-values for Log-Rank test are 0.00004 and &lt;0.00001, respectively. For female mice, the p-values for Likelihood Ratio test are &lt;0.00001 and &lt;0.00001 and the p-values for Log-Rank test are &lt;0.00001 and &lt;0.00001, respectively.
L1652: Tumor analysis showed that no GBT440-related clinical pathology findings suggested an effect on the occurrence of neoplasia. There were significant tumor findings for the positive control group when compared with vehicle or water control groups:
L1653: - For male mice, the pairwise comparisons between the vehicle control and the positive control showed statistically significant increases in incidence of Lymphoma in Hemolympho-reticu (P-value&lt;0.001), Squamous cell Papilloma in Stomach, nonglandu (P-value&lt;0.001), Squamous cell Carcinoma in Stomach, nonglandu (P-value=0.0071), Combined tumors of Squamous cell Papilloma+Carcinoma in Skin/Subcutis (P-value=0.0423).
- For male mice, the pairwise comparisons between the water control and the positive control showed statistically significant increases in incidence of Lymphoma in Hemolympho-reticu (P-value&lt;0.001), Squamous cell Papilloma in Stomach, nonglandu (P-value&lt;0.001), Squamous cell Carcinoma in Stomach, nonglandu (P-value=0.0071), Combined tumors of Squamous cell Papilloma+Carcinoma in Skin/Subcutis (P-value=0.0423).
- For female mice, the pairwise comparisons between the vehicle control and the positive control showed statistically significant increases in incidence of Lymphoma in Hemolympho-reticu (P-value&lt;0.001), Squamous cell Papilloma in Stomach, nonglandu (P-value&lt;0.001), Squamous cell Papilloma in Skin/Subcutis (P-value=0.0345), Combined tumors of Squamous cell Papilloma+Carcinoma in Skin/Subcutis (P-value=0.0345).
- For female mice, the pairwise comparisons between the water control and the positive control showed statistically significant increases in incidence of Lymphoma in Hemolympho-reticu (P-value&lt;0.001), Squamous cell Papilloma in Stomach, nonglandu (P-value&lt;0.001), Squamous cell Papilloma in Skin/Subcutis (P-value=0.0369), Combined tumors of Squamous cell
L1654: Papilloma+Carcinoma in Skin/Subcutis (P-value=0.0369).

L1703: Figure 1 displays two panels showing the dose-response relationship of Voxelotor (mg) on pharmacodynamic markers p20 (top panel) and p50 (bottom panel). Both panels show a dose-dependent decrease in the respective partial pressure values as the Voxelotor dose increases from 0 mg to 1000 mg. Error bars are included for all data points.
Top Panel: p20 (mm Hg) vs Voxelotor (mg)
Voxelotor (mg)	p20 (mm Hg)
0	18.5
400	15.0
600	14.0
1000	10.0
Bottom Panel: p50 (mm Hg) vs Voxelotor (mg)
Voxelotor (mg)	p50 (mm Hg)
0	35.0
400	31.0
600	30.0
1000	28.0
L1704: Figure 1: Dose-Response Relationship of Voxelotor and Pharmacodynamic Markers. The graph plots % Hb Mod (Y-axis, 0 to 40) against Voxelotor dose (mg) (X-axis, 0 to 1000). The data points show a dose-dependent increase in % Hb Mod, ranging from approximately 0% at 0 mg to approximately 25% at 1000 mg, with error bars indicating variability.
L1705: Figure 2 is a line graph illustrating the time course of the Least Squares (LS) Mean (95% CI) Change from Baseline in Hemoglobin (Hb, g/dL) over 24 weeks for three treatment groups: Voxelotor 1500 mg (n=88), Voxelotor 900 mg (n=92), and Placebo (n=91).
The Y-axis represents the LS Mean (95% CI) Change from Baseline in Hb, g/dL, ranging from -0.5 to 2.0. The X-axis represents Weeks (0, 4, 8, 12, 16, 20, 24).
At Week 0, all groups start at a change of 0 g/dL.
By Week 4, Voxelotor 1500 mg shows a significant increase, reaching approximately 1.4 g/dL. Voxelotor 900 mg shows a smaller increase, reaching approximately 0.8 g/dL. Placebo remains near 0 g/dL.
From Week 4 to Week 24, both Voxelotor groups maintain their respective increases. Voxelotor 1500 mg stabilizes around 1.1 g/dL, and Voxelotor 900 mg stabilizes around 0.6 g/dL. Placebo remains near 0 g/dL.
The graph demonstrates that both Voxelotor doses significantly increase Hb levels compared to placebo over the 24-week period.
Figure 2. Time Course of Mean Hemoglobin Change from Baseline.
L1706: Scatter plot showing CFB Hemoglobin (g/dL) on the Y-axis versus voxelotor whole blood AUC (\mu\text{g}\cdot\text{h}/\text{mL}) on the X-axis. Data points are categorized by dosage (placebo, 900 mg, 1500 mg) and genotype (GBT440-007, GBT440-031). A positive linear trend is observed, with a P-value of <0.001. Below the scatter plot, a box plot illustrates the distribution of voxelotor whole blood AUC for the three dosage groups (placebo, 900 mg, 1500 mg). The green dotted vertical line indicates the median predicted AUC for 900 mg QD dosage, and the purple vertical dotted line indicates the median predicted AUC for 1500 mg dosage.
pkpd-wk24-hgb-20190508.pdf
L1707: Figure 4 displays two exposure-response plots showing the relationship between average voxelotor plasma Cmax (\mu\text{g/mL}) and the probability of specific adverse events.
Top Plot: Probability of ALT increased (%) vs. average voxelotor plasma Cmax (\mu\text{g/mL})
Y-axis: Probability of ALT increased (%), ranging from 0 to 100.
X-axis: average voxelotor plasma Cmax (\mu\text{g/mL}), ranging from 0 to 20.
Data points are shown for Yes (N=71, triangles) and No (N=166, circles).
The fitted curve shows a positive relationship, with a p-value of p<0.001.

L1724: Figure 16 displays two box plots showing changes from baseline in vital signs for the Safety Population (Study GBT440-031).
The left plot (GR0009) shows the maximum change from baseline in Diastolic Blood Pressure (DBP). The Y-axis ranges from -10 to 45. The right plot (GR0011) shows the minimum change from baseline in DBP. The Y-axis ranges from -35 to 15.
Both plots compare three treatment arms: GBT440 1500 mg (Blue), GBT440 900 mg (Orange), and Placebo (Green).
In the GR0009 plot (Max Change from Baseline), the Placebo group shows the highest median change (around 28), followed by the 900 mg group (around 16), and the 1500 mg group (around 18). All groups show a range of changes, with the 1500 mg group having the largest interquartile range (IQR) extending up to 40.
In the GR0011 plot (Min Change from Baseline), the Placebo group shows the lowest median change (around -5), followed by the 900 mg group (around -5), and the 1500 mg group (around -5). All groups show a range of changes, with the 1500 mg group having the largest IQR extending down to -30.
Overall, the plots suggest that the maximum change in DBP was highest in the Placebo group, while the minimum change was lowest in the Placebo group, with minimal differences observed between the active treatment arms.
